## Detailed Assessments

### Hitachi Energy India Limited (POWERINDIA) - 8.55 / 10 - HIGH
*Sector: Power Transmission / Automation - Confidence: High - Updated: 2026-09-04*

**Summary:** High trust: Hitachi-controlled, MNC-grade governance, debt-free balance sheet, ~29% ROCE and outstanding execution with a record ₹32,222 Cr backlog. Minor: parent related-party reliance and a premium valuation.

| Pillar (weight) | Score | Rationale |
|---|:---:|---|
| Promoter Profile (20%) | 9 | Hitachi (Japan) global infrastructure major; ex ABB Power Grids JV, now 71.31% Hitachi Energy-controlled. Strong parent technology and governance; MD & CEO N Venu long-tenured. |
| Ownership Behaviour (25%) | 8 | Promoter 75% to 71.31% via a value-accretive QIP (~₹2,295 Cr, FY25); holding unchanged at 71.31% through Jun-26 SHP; no pledge, orderly. |
| Capital Allocation (20%) | 8 | Almost debt-free (borrowings ₹86 Cr at Mar-26), FY26 ROCE 29.4%/ROE 21.9%, CFO ₹1,245 Cr vs PAT ₹988 Cr, FCF ₹735 Cr; reinvesting via ₹4,000 Cr total capex incl. ₹2,000 Cr Karjan (Vadodara) large power transformer plant started Jun-2026; low dividend (4% payout) acceptable for a growth MNC. |
| Governance (20%) | 9 | MNC-grade controls, clean disclosures, no red flags; material RPTs with Hitachi Energy Sweden/USA/Switzerland put to shareholder postal ballot (board 28 Aug 2026); 7th AGM passed all resolutions 28 Aug 2026; CRISIL ESG score up to 69 from 61 (2 Sep 2026). |
| Execution (15%) | 9 | 5-yr PAT CAGR ~54%; OPM expanded 7% (FY24) to 16% (FY26); Q1 FY27 (7 Aug 2026) revenue +69%, PAT +123%, orders +26% ex-HVDC, record backlog ₹32,222 Cr, first BESS order won. |

*Sources: screener.in (Jun-26 SHP, FY26 financials), Q1 FY27 press release 7 Aug 2026 (hitachienergy.com), BSE filings (AGM/RPT postal ballot 28 Aug 2026; CRISIL ESG 2 Sep 2026), Investor Day May-2026 capex disclosure (ScanX), CRISIL*

---

### Narayana Hrudayalaya Limited (NH) - 8.35 / 10 - HIGH
*Sector: Healthcare - Confidence: High - Updated: 2026-09-04*

**Summary:** High-trust, founder-led healthcare franchise with clean governance, stable promoter holding and strong, consistent execution. Main watch: leverage stepped up (gross borrowings ₹5,858 Cr at Mar-26) for the UK acquisition and India expansion; net debt/equity still a comfortable 0.42 (Jun-26).

| Pillar (weight) | Score | Rationale |

| Ownership Behaviour (25%) | 3 | Promoter holding fell from 59.83% (Mar-24) to 50.55% (Sep-24) and 46.28% (Mar-26) via dilutive issuances including the Feb-2026 ₹900 Cr preferential issue; 60% of promoter shares pledged per the Jun-26 SHP, and on 11 Jun 2026 GMR Business & Consultancy LLP pledged a further 2.55 Cr shares (3.27% of capital) against ₹1,400 Cr of promoter debt with security cover of only 0.19x. No dividend has ever been paid. |
| Capital Allocation (20%) | 4 | ROCE 12% (FY26) and interest of ₹1,659 Cr roughly equal to operating profit of ₹1,604 Cr; net worth recovery came from arbitration settlements (₹1,083 Cr GKEL), divestments (₹653 Cr) and equity (₹900 Cr) rather than operating returns. Positives: corporate debt reduced, gearing down to 9.09x from 56.37x, and Infomerics notes calibrated capex; but smart-meter debt rose ₹580 Cr in Q1 FY27 and FY27 maturities (₹1,489 Cr) exceed FY26 CFO (₹1,260 Cr). |
| Governance (20%) | 4 | Complex group structure with related-party funding (Infomerics: ~57% of FY27 scheduled debt repayments pertain to promoter/group companies), contingent liabilities ₹2,917 Cr, promoter pledges and no payout. Mitigants: regular quarterly concalls (Aug 2026, May 2026), detailed segment disclosure, Bosch partnership with 10% SPV equity, Infomerics upgrade to IVR BBB/Stable (29 May 2026) and NCDs repaid in full (Feb-2026); 7th AGM notice issued 27 Aug 2026 for 21 Sep 2026. |
| Execution (15%) | 5 | Operationally sound — PLFs of 87-90% versus ~77% industry, Ambala-Chandigarh traffic +5.8% YoY, plant-level net debt down (Kamalanga ₹2,069 Cr vs ₹2,403 Cr; Warora ₹1,894 Cr vs ₹2,278 Cr YoY) and 4.1 mn smart meters installed by 31 Jul 2026. But smart-meter milestones had to be extended to Mar-2028, segment EBITDA was negative in Q1 FY27 and the group reported net losses of ₹35 Cr (Q1 FY27), ₹114 Cr (Q4 FY26) and ₹160 Cr (Q3 FY26). |

*Sources: screener.in consolidated (FY26 financials, SHP to Jun-26, pledge 60%, announcements 26-28 Aug 2026); Infomerics rating rationale 29 May 2026; Q1 FY27 results/presentation analysis (Multibagg, 15 Aug 2026); SAST pledge disclosure 11 Jun 2026 (Whalesbook 16 Jun 2026; InvestorFeed); ScanX note on Infomerics reaffirmation/NCD withdrawal (Feb-2026)*
